# Iteration 6 — Map C: `skill:system-deep-loop` (787) + `skill:cli-external-orchestration` (756)

**Run:** 6 | **Focus:** Map C for the two largest remaining skill areas. Full per-file tables in `working/map-c--skill_system-deep-loop--*.md` and `working/map-c--skill_cli-external-orchestration--*.md`; reproduced below.

| area | files | seed | class totals |
|---|---|---|---|
| skill:system-deep-loop | 787 | 787 ✓ | mechanical ~600 / freeze ~180 / manual 3 |
| skill:cli-external-orchestration | 756 | 756 ✓ | mechanical ~380 / freeze ~376 / manual 0 |
| **iter total** | **1543** | **1543** ✓ | freeze 569 · manual 3 · mechanical 971 |

Combined Map C progress: 729 + 1543 = **2272 / 4258**.

## Key findings

1. **`check-contract-drift.cjs` joins the contract family.** It pattern-matches `.opencode/` authority paths (`RELATIVE_PATH_PATTERN`, `startsWith('.opencode/')` at :143,:165,:194,:236) — a governance gate whose semantics depend on the source-root name, not just its constants. `manual`. Same for `check-agent-mirror-sync.cjs` + `mirror-sync-verify.cjs` (the self-disengaging mirror gate + its verify lib, phase-001).
2. **`resolve-authority-root.ts:70`** constructs `.opencode/skills/.state/authority` — a path constant, `mechanical`, but note the *existing* `.state` content is derived state (regenerates; the old tree's state stays behind unless migrated).
3. **cli-external-orchestration carries zero code refs.** All 756 files are `md` docs or `json`/`csv` benchmark artifacts under nested `cli-*/benchmark/reports/` dirs — the per-CLI subareas (`cli-codex` 134, `cli-opencode` 175, …) are doc surfaces + frozen run reports. `freeze` dominates (~376) because every `cli-*/benchmark/reports/**` report is a past run.
4. **Content-addressed cache files** (`deep-improvement/scripts/model-benchmark/scorer/cache/grader/*.out.md`) are hashed grader outputs — `freeze` (a past run's record; the cache key already won't match after paths change).
5. **deep-loop `tests/` (67) and `test-fixtures/`** — assertions/fixture content, `mechanical`; no snaps in this area.
6. **Doc-heavy surface**: ~900 of the 1543 files are `md` — runnable instructions in fences (`mechanical`), prose (`mechanical`), changelog history (`freeze`).

## Ruled out

- `cli-*` json rows that are *not* under `benchmark/` — only `permissions-matrix.example-*.json` + `graph-metadata.json` (config data, `mechanical`).
- No `__snapshots__`, no `assets/compiled`, no `.state` doc rows in either skill.
